## Latest handoff — AUTH-02 SignalR expiry-aware token factory (2026-11-10)

The active PR #143 uses getSignalRAccessToken for the Rider selected-ride hub and all three Driver hubs. A finite future JWT exp is used directly. Expired or locally unverifiable tokens go through the existing epoch-guarded apiFetch('/api/v1/auth/me') path, which owns refresh and session invalidation; the helper re-reads SecureStore with no stale in-memory fallback and rechecks the captured epoch. AUTH-LOGIN-050–056 add seven deterministic cases, including overflow expiry and logout during SecureStore read or server validation.

CI #1145 / 42805017563 failed 11/12 because Rider iOS simctl install exceeded the 180-second bound after a 2m41 first-boot migration. The simulator harness now defaults individual operations to 300 seconds (maximum remains 300) while first-boot bootstatus remains separately bounded at 600 seconds. RELEASE-SMOKE-026 verifies the default. Expected suite totals after this increment: shared JS 136, Python 41; backend remains 373. Exact-head CI and all four same-run simulator artifact inspections are pending.

Do not merge PR #143 until exact-head CI is green, all four simulator artifacts are inspected, and final review/policy checks pass. Live TriSend rotation/reuse and signed-device SecureStore/Keychain acceptance remain external.

## Latest sequential handoff — RIDER-02 proposal submission ownership hardening (2026-11-11)

PR #145 remains the sole active feature PR on `fix/rider-02-proposal-idempotency`. Current source/test head: `56a363621029fbfb16e2ef7c72d14f1faad48c8d`.

A deeper review found that the original boolean proposal in-flight guard could remain set after account reset/ride change until an old network promise settled. Its unconditional `finally` could also release a newer submission. Both Rider and Driver now use an attempt-key-owned lock. `finishProposalSubmission(ref, expectedKey)` only clears a matching owner; session reset and ride identity change clear the old attempt/lock and set submitting UI state false. The old finalizer is harmless if a replacement attempt owns the lock.

New deterministic coverage:
- `a stale proposal completion cannot release a newer in-flight submission`.
- Rider/Driver source-integration assertions verify lock ownership, session reset cleanup and ride-change cleanup.
- New test-plan cases CAB-AGR-029 and CAB-AGR-030.
- Expected shared JavaScript suite: 153 tests.

Earlier CI #1168 passed 12/12 before the actor/currency and lock-ownership additions. CI #1171 / 42879001921 was on superseded head `0a7bb547...`; it must not be reused. Fresh exact-head CI on `56a363621029fbfb16e2ef7c72d14f1faad48c8d` and all same-run native/simulator artifact inspection are required. PR #145 is not merged.

The pending proposal attempt remains in-memory; replay across process death is not proven. Live Cosmos ETag/multi-instance and physical-device negotiation/reconnect remain external gates.

Next after PR #145 merges: re-read main and implement #5 RIDER-03 Rider-scoped active-booking reservation across different Idempotency-Keys, using the existing rider-partitioned `RideIdempotency` storage only after verifying partition/ETag/transaction semantics. Strict acceptance remains 0/19 (15 In Progress, 4 Blocked); production readiness is not established.

## Current RIDER-03 handoff — 2026-11-11

Main HEAD: `ab988b02b01d89394e5213586f9ee24f0e78c6b1` (RIDER-02 PR #145 merged). Active draft PR #146: `feat/rider-03-single-active-booking`, head `7e49cce79698fe5c107d8d8014e5eca61dd54d56`; exact-head CI #1199 is pending.

### Rider-scoped reservation design
- Reuse the existing Cosmos `RideIdempotency` container, partition key `/riderId`; reservation document ID is `active-selected-booking`. No new container or migration.
- Create-if-absent reservation and privacy-minimised audit event are written in one same-partition transactional batch. Permanent promotion and matching release are ETag-conditional transactional batches with append-only audit.
- In-memory implementation is atomic and uses an injectable clock for deterministic expiry tests. It never steals an expired reservation; the API reconciles the authoritative ride first.
- The booking route reserves the Rider before acquiring the selected Driver lease and persisting the ride. Same RideId/request-hash retries are idempotent; a different active RideId returns HTTP 409.
- Negotiating/AgreementConfirmed leases remain temporary. Driver acceptance promotes the Rider reservation to permanent. Cancellation, Driver decline, expiry and completion release only the matching RiderId+RideId. Terminal read-repair handles interrupted release.
- Same-key replay repairs a missing reservation and returns the existing ride. Acceptance can repair a pre-deployment selected ride only if no different Rider reservation exists and the request fingerprint reconstructed from the ride matches.
- Rider app loads server-owned active ride state after sign-in, redirects to Activity and blocks Book/discovery/request until terminal state. Backend enforcement is authoritative.

### Deterministic evidence added (pending CI)
- Store unit tests: atomic different-Ride acquisition, same-Ride/hash replay, independent Rider partitions, permanent promotion, exact/stale release, expired no-steal and audit privacy.
- API integration tests: one Rider/two Drivers/different keys, independent Riders, cancellation/rebooking, accepted promotion and same-key replay, completion release, expired-negotiation reconciliation and Driver-decline release.
- Mobile shared tests: active status helper and source integration for sign-in hydration, Book tab guard, discovery guard and request guard.

### External gates
Live Cosmos transactional-batch/ETag/multi-instance/restart validation and Android/iOS physical-device booking/cancel/expiry acceptance remain unverified. Production `Dispatch:SelectedDriverNegotiationLifetimeMinutes` is unset; selected booking correctly returns 503 until Product approves/configures the value. The 15-minute test fixture is not a product default.

Next after merge: re-read main, audit #6 DISPATCH-01 in order, implement all safe gaps, then proceed to DISPATCH-02. Do not open a second feature PR while this one is active.

## Latest sequential handoff — DISPATCH-04 historical active-ride fallback (2026-11-12)

- Base main before PR #154: 3b267fc1d1f7b1eb5e200fb73d9bb5e6c6b643fa. PR #154 code-bearing head 02fbfa9121b62acf5ef8ec20287cc9dc0ea94db5; exact-head CI #1330 / 43178946882 passed 12/12 (backend 477/477, shared mobile 179/179, Python 46/46, both app validations/bundles, native API publish, two Android release APKs, two unsigned iOS Simulator builds and four emulator/simulator smokes).
- Implementation covers historical active ride aggregates even when reservations are absent; all known active states including legacy Matching/Offered; terminal/orphan reservation cleanup followed by a second active-ride check; same-ride idempotency with a separate active ride; Driver acceptance; batched discovery and reservation reads (100 IDs per query); exact Cosmos item ID/embedded RideId checks; owner-bound Rider/Driver history; and authoritative expiry reconciliation that re-reads after ETag/CAS conflict and fails closed on missing/mismatched/unknown latest state.
- Test-plan cases DISPATCH-04-LEGACY-001–008 are implemented. Query contracts verify the minimal identity/status/expiry projection and the reservation-document ID predicate that excludes append-only audit records. No new customer-facing FIFO, auto-assignment, non-Cab workflow, fare/payment, schema migration, secret or container change.

- External gates: live Cosmos cross-partition query/index/RU/partition behaviour, multi-instance ETag/CAS contention and restart/crash recovery; Product-approved negotiation lifetime/radius/zones/location freshness; Android/iOS concurrent booking/availability/transfer acceptance. After PR #154 integration, continue to #18 PLATFORM-01; do not stop because #15 DRIVER-04 or #17 SUPPORT-01 require Product/Operations decisions.


## Latest sequential handoff — PLATFORM-01 reservation audit privacy contract (2026-11-12)

- DISPATCH-04 PR #154 merged at `09a807e1024bf386450713c0645221ff9002f040` after exact documentation-head CI #1339 / 43188534570 passed 12/12 on `209ccc219a3ac63a74ff6e37fdbb0b159e9c8399`. - Current focused branch: `fix/platform-01-reservation-audit-privacy-contract`. Enforce exactly four Driver reservation audit event types; reject unknown/malformed values before event creation. ## Supplemental AI-01 source audit (2026-11-14)

- `AiAssistanceEndpoints.cs` and `AiAssistanceService.cs` expose Rider trip-intent draft and Rider/Driver approved-content help only. The provider boundary remains an unavailable placeholder; no paid provider/model/credential is selected.
- Options validate bounded timeout/input/output/rate limits and require an HTTPS endpoint plus managed credential reference if enabled. Endpoints require Rider/Driver roles and use a per-subject fixed-window rate limiter.
- Trip-intent provider prompts remove the full route span and send only generic allow-listed words plus `[PICKUP]`/`[DROPOFF]` tokens. Locally parsed pickup/dropoff and passenger count remain authoritative; every draft requires user confirmation.
- Help provider prompts contain only allow-listed keywords and approved source IDs; the service returns in-repository help content, never provider-authored answer text. Unknown/ambiguous questions escalate.
- Service tests cover provider timeout, cancellation, late faults, HTTP/unexpected failures, redaction, allow-listed content, output/input bounds and deterministic fallback; API integration tests cover role authorization and per-subject rate limiting. The current backend suite includes these tests.
- Live provider/model/credential selection and provider validation remain external. Optional Driver offer explanation is not required for the 19-row denominator. AI-01 is supplemental and is not counted as an acceptance row.

## Latest engineering handoff — RIDER-02 PR #169 (2026-11-15)

- Main HEAD: `33c863cec4155c116a2089cd9804e54ca6fad5a4`. RIDER-01 PR #168 merged after exact-head CI #1713 / run 43712409000 passed 12/12.
- Sole active PR: [#169](https://github.com/rajeevp727/turnova-platform/pull/169), branch `feat/rider-02-persistent-proposal-idempotency`; source/test head `0c6a5b89d1542121980a96221a2edc277c3374f7`; exact-head CI pending.
- Both Rider and Driver persist a versioned proposal attempt in Expo SecureStore before POST. The slot is scoped by actor and authenticated user; the validated fingerprint includes ride ID, amount and currency. Same terms after process restart reuse the same Idempotency-Key; changed terms get a new key.
- SecureStore read/write failure fails closed before proposal POST. Per-store mutation serialization prevents concurrent creation races. The attempt remains through ambiguous POST/history-refresh failure and is cleared only after successful history refresh. Cleanup compares both idempotency key and fingerprint, so a stale completion cannot clear a newer attempt even if the key string collides; failed deletion retains the key for safe replay. Rider/Driver in-flight locks remain unique pending tokens independent of Idempotency-Key values. Session reset clears in-memory refs/locks but retains the durable user/actor record for same-account retry after re-authentication; other accounts cannot restore it. Ride changes clear the old matching record.
- Fourteen deterministic persistent-store tests plus Rider/Driver source-integration assertions are included; invalid-envelope cases include cross-account/actor slot mismatch. CI must verify backend, shared mobile tests, both Expo apps, four native builds, API publish and four same-run smoke jobs on the exact final PR head before merge.
- External: live Cosmos ETag/multi-instance/restart; signed-device SecureStore/Keychain; Android/iOS physical-device negotiation/confirmation/renegotiation/reconnect. Strict acceptance remains 0/19 (15 In Progress, 4 Blocked); production readiness is not established.


### RIDER-02 corrupt-record safety correction — 2026-11-15

Follow-up source/test commit `0c6a5b89d1542121980a96221a2edc277c3374f7` distinguishes missing, valid and invalid SecureStore proposal records. Invalid JSON, unsupported version, malformed fields, fingerprint inconsistency or a valid record stored under a different authenticated-user/actor slot now fail closed before a new key is generated or any proposal POST is sent. This prevents overwriting an unreadable key that might represent a server-accepted ambiguous proposal. A corrupt record is deleted only after successful agreement-history refresh if the current in-memory attempt still owns the expected key. CAB-AGR-038 covers the no-new-key/no-request behavior. Exact-head CI must be rerun; the prior CI run is stale after this source change.


### RIDER-02 async session-race follow-up — 2026-11-15

Source/test commit `0c6a5b89d1542121980a96221a2edc277c3374f7` adds CAB-AGR-039–041 for session invalidation during SecureStore read/write and ownership-checked cleanup of corrupt records after successful agreement-history refresh. CAB-AGR-038 also verifies valid records copied into another authenticated-user or actor slot fail closed without overwrite. CAB-AGR-042 verifies same-key/different-fingerprint stale cleanup cannot clear a newer attempt and the app submission lock is independent of the idempotency key. The shared suite now has 14 persistent proposal-attempt cases plus Rider/Driver source-integration assertions. CI #1717 was already running against the prior head and is stale for these added tests; exact final-head CI is required before merge.

## PR #192 concurrency review follow-up — 2026-11-18

The Activity action lock now uses unique owner tokens containing the captured session epoch and a monotonically increasing sequence. This prevents an old request's `finally` from releasing a new action after a session reset even when both requests target the same ride and agreement version. RIDER-ACTIVITY-ACTION-006 covers the reset/reacquire collision. The change remains client-side; API/server lifecycle contracts are unchanged. Final documentation-bearing head `10d26aae635685c8fe864483786671f956587198` passed [CI #44290908866](https://github.com/rajeevp727/turnova-platform/actions/runs/44290908866), all 9 required API/Android jobs; 3 iOS jobs were skipped. Mobile npm audit inventory on both apps: 34 vulnerable package nodes total, 0 critical, 27 high nodes, 7 moderate, 0 low. The high nodes map to the inherited GHSA-fc5q-39xc-vjpf picomatch ReDoS advisory through transitive Expo CLI/Metro/Jest build tooling; it is absent from app imports and exported Android bundles. It is not patched or treated as a clean audit. npm offers remediation only through major Expo 58.0.26 / React Native 0.88.1 upgrades from the current Expo 54 / RN 0.81 baseline; keep that compatibility/security upgrade as a separate, fully tested increment. Recheck current PR state and repository merge policy; do not self-approve or bypass protection. Current phase remains Android Rider first; Driver and iOS are still gated.

## API response minimization follow-up — 2026-11-19

The Rider app's `Ride` contract and selected-booking flow do not consume `RiderId` or `DriverId` from the booking response. PR #195 therefore removes those two actor identifiers from `RiderSelectedBookingResponse`; the Rider already supplied the selected Driver in the booking request. API-PRIV-001 now asserts that the booking response omits both actor IDs. This is a response-only minimization; booking identity, ownership authorization, reservation and idempotency behavior are unchanged.


## Current API response-minimization checkpoint — 2026-11-19

RiderSelectedBookingResponse no longer echoes RiderId or DriverId. The current Rider app sends the selected Driver ID in its booking request and does not consume actor IDs from the response. API-PRIV-001 asserts both properties are absent. Code-bearing privacy correction `b699453202064fcdb99ba4cf66cdbbb78a96ed07`; test-only compatibility correction `c211fa03f7b40e042376c0d701efa988b17f684b` passed exact-head CI #44513190938 (763/763, native publish, verifier 11 negative fixtures plus valid fixture, artifact 14523623180). PR #195 remains open and requires independent human approval; do not self-approve or merge without approval.


## Latest API test handoff — 2026-11-19

CI #44512525481 failed 762/763 because `SelectedBookingReservationPreventsConcurrentBookingsAndRiderCancelReleasesIt` still depended on `riderId` in the selected-booking response. The test now asserts `riderId`/`driverId` absence and reads the owner from the test-only in-memory `DemoRide` store before checking cancellation audit. Production API code is unchanged by this correction. Fresh exact-head CI is required; do not merge or advance to Android until it passes and independent human approval is recorded.
